 Ask 
 On this page 

 API 

 Cortex XSOAR 

 XSOAR 6.x APIs 

 Cortex XSOAR 6.x APIs 

 Playbooks 

 APIs for managing playbooks 

 Add ad-hoc task 

 post https://hostname /inv-playbook/task/add/ {investigationId} 

 Add an ad-hoc task to a running playbook 

 Authorizations 

 api_key 

 Authorization string Required 

 Path parameters 

 investigationId string Required 

 investigation ID 

 Body 

 application/json 

 addAfter boolean Optional 

 addToSeparateBranch boolean Optional 

 automationScript string Optional 

 description string Optional 

 loop object · TaskLoop Optional 

 TaskLoop contains loop details 

 Show properties 

 name string Optional 

 neighborInvPBTaskId string Optional 

 playbookId string Optional 

 scriptArguments object Optional 

 Show properties 

 separateContext boolean Optional 

 tags string[] Optional 

 type string Optional 

 TaskType is the Task in the playbook context as a node 

 Responses 

 200 

 InvestigationPlaybook 

 application/json 

 InvestigationPlaybook is a wrapper for a linked list of Playbook tasks 

 Dirty boolean Optional 

 ReadyPlaybookInputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 ReplacedPlaybook boolean Optional 

 Indicate whether this playbook has new history during this session 

 ShardID integer · int64 Optional 

 UpdatedOperatorIDs boolean Optional 

 allRead boolean Optional 

 allReadWrite boolean Optional 

 autoExtracting boolean Optional 

 cacheVersn integer · int64 Optional 

 comment string Optional 

 created string · date-time Optional 

 dbotCreatedBy string Optional 

 Who has created this event - relevant only for manual incidents 

 hasRole boolean Optional 

 Internal field to make queries on role faster 

 highlight object Optional 

 Show properties 

 id string Optional 

 incidentCreateDate string · date-time Optional 

 Incident create date 

 indexName string Optional 

 inputs object · PlaybookInput[] Optional 

 PlaybookInputs - array of PlaybookInput 

 Show properties 

 invPBDebugInfo object · InvPlaybookDebugInfo ... Optional 

 Show properties 

 investigationId string Optional 

 isTIM boolean Optional 

 modified string · date-time Optional 

 name string Optional 

 numericId integer · int64 Optional 

 outputs object · PlaybookOutput[] Optional 

 PlaybookOutputs - array of PlaybookOutput 

 Show properties 

 pbHistory object · InvestigationPlaybookData[] Optional 

 in: body 

 Show properties 

 pendingTasks object Optional 

 Tasks that are marked for running, but cannot yet run due to incomplete parents 

 Show properties 

 playbookId string Optional 

 previousAllRead boolean Optional 

 previousAllReadWrite boolean Optional 

 previousRoles string[] Optional 

 Do not change this field manually 

 primaryTerm integer · int64 Optional 

 quiet boolean Optional 

 recoveryAttempts integer · int64 Optional 

 roles string[] Optional 

 The role assigned to this investigation 

 sequenceNumber integer · int64 Optional 

 serverId string Optional 

 Holds the ID of the responsible cluster app server 

 sizeInBytes integer · int64 Optional 

 sortValues string[] Optional 

 startDate string · date-time Optional 

 startTaskId string Optional 

 FirstTask is the root task of the playbook 

 state string Optional 

 InvestigationPlaybookState indicates the state of the running playbook 

 subPlaybookInputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 subPlaybookOutputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 syncHash string Optional 

 tasks object Optional 

 Show properties 

 version integer · int64 Optional 

 view object · PlaybookView Optional 

 PlaybookView represents the view in client of playbook graph 

 xsoarHasReadOnlyRole boolean Optional 

 xsoarPreviousReadOnlyRoles string[] Optional 

 xsoarReadOnlyRoles string[] Optional 

 post /inv-playbook/task/add/ {investigationId} 

 HTTP 

 Ask Copy

 Accept: */*

 Assign task 

 post https://hostname /inv-playbook/task/assign 

 Assign a task to an owner 

 Authorizations 

 api_key 

 Authorization string Required 

 Body 

 application/json 

 assignee string Optional 

 inTaskID string Optional 

 invId string Optional 

 version integer · int64 Optional 

 Responses 

 200 

 InvestigationPlaybook 

 application/json 

 InvestigationPlaybook is a wrapper for a linked list of Playbook tasks 

 Dirty boolean Optional 

 ReadyPlaybookInputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 ReplacedPlaybook boolean Optional 

 Indicate whether this playbook has new history during this session 

 ShardID integer · int64 Optional 

 UpdatedOperatorIDs boolean Optional 

 allRead boolean Optional 

 allReadWrite boolean Optional 

 autoExtracting boolean Optional 

 cacheVersn integer · int64 Optional 

 comment string Optional 

 created string · date-time Optional 

 dbotCreatedBy string Optional 

 Who has created this event - relevant only for manual incidents 

 hasRole boolean Optional 

 Internal field to make queries on role faster 

 highlight object Optional 

 Show properties 

 id string Optional 

 incidentCreateDate string · date-time Optional 

 Incident create date 

 indexName string Optional 

 inputs object · PlaybookInput[] Optional 

 PlaybookInputs - array of PlaybookInput 

 Show properties 

 invPBDebugInfo object · InvPlaybookDebugInfo ... Optional 

 Show properties 

 investigationId string Optional 

 isTIM boolean Optional 

 modified string · date-time Optional 

 name string Optional 

 numericId integer · int64 Optional 

 outputs object · PlaybookOutput[] Optional 

 PlaybookOutputs - array of PlaybookOutput 

 Show properties 

 pbHistory object · InvestigationPlaybookData[] Optional 

 in: body 

 Show properties 

 pendingTasks object Optional 

 Tasks that are marked for running, but cannot yet run due to incomplete parents 

 Show properties 

 playbookId string Optional 

 previousAllRead boolean Optional 

 previousAllReadWrite boolean Optional 

 previousRoles string[] Optional 

 Do not change this field manually 

 primaryTerm integer · int64 Optional 

 quiet boolean Optional 

 recoveryAttempts integer · int64 Optional 

 roles string[] Optional 

 The role assigned to this investigation 

 sequenceNumber integer · int64 Optional 

 serverId string Optional 

 Holds the ID of the responsible cluster app server 

 sizeInBytes integer · int64 Optional 

 sortValues string[] Optional 

 startDate string · date-time Optional 

 startTaskId string Optional 

 FirstTask is the root task of the playbook 

 state string Optional 

 InvestigationPlaybookState indicates the state of the running playbook 

 subPlaybookInputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 subPlaybookOutputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 syncHash string Optional 

 tasks object Optional 

 Show properties 

 version integer · int64 Optional 

 view object · PlaybookView Optional 

 PlaybookView represents the view in client of playbook graph 

 xsoarHasReadOnlyRole boolean Optional 

 xsoarPreviousReadOnlyRoles string[] Optional 

 xsoarReadOnlyRoles string[] Optional 

 post /inv-playbook/task/assign 

 HTTP

 Complete task simple (no file) 

 post https://hostname /inv-playbook/task/complete/simple 

 Complete a task without a file attachment 

 Authorizations 

 api_key 

 Authorization string Required 

 Body 

 application/json 

 args object Optional 

 Show properties 

 comment string Optional 

 conditions object · TaskCondition[] Optional 

 TaskCondition - contains a condition and a corresponding label
if the condition satisfied - its label is taken as the next task(s) input (e.i. the label on the edge) 

 Show properties 

 inTaskID string Optional 

 input string Optional 

 invId string Optional 

 loopArgs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 loopCondition object · ArgAtomicFilter[][] Optional 

 ArgFilter - represent a slice of atomic filters with OR condition between them (e.i. - atomic1 OR atomic2 OR ...) 

 Show properties 

 version integer · int64 Optional 

 Responses 

 200 

 InvestigationPlaybook 

 application/json 

 InvestigationPlaybook is a wrapper for a linked list of Playbook tasks 

 Dirty boolean Optional 

 ReadyPlaybookInputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 ReplacedPlaybook boolean Optional 

 Indicate whether this playbook has new history during this session 

 ShardID integer · int64 Optional 

 UpdatedOperatorIDs boolean Optional 

 allRead boolean Optional 

 allReadWrite boolean Optional 

 autoExtracting boolean Optional 

 cacheVersn integer · int64 Optional 

 comment string Optional 

 created string · date-time Optional 

 dbotCreatedBy string Optional 

 Who has created this event - relevant only for manual incidents 

 hasRole boolean Optional 

 Internal field to make queries on role faster 

 highlight object Optional 

 Show properties 

 id string Optional 

 incidentCreateDate string · date-time Optional 

 Incident create date 

 indexName string Optional 

 inputs object · PlaybookInput[] Optional 

 PlaybookInputs - array of PlaybookInput 

 Show properties 

 invPBDebugInfo object · InvPlaybookDebugInfo ... Optional 

 Show properties 

 investigationId string Optional 

 isTIM boolean Optional 

 modified string · date-time Optional 

 name string Optional 

 numericId integer · int64 Optional 

 outputs object · PlaybookOutput[] Optional 

 PlaybookOutputs - array of PlaybookOutput 

 Show properties 

 pbHistory object · InvestigationPlaybookData[] Optional 

 in: body 

 Show properties 

 pendingTasks object Optional 

 Tasks that are marked for running, but cannot yet run due to incomplete parents 

 Show properties 

 playbookId string Optional 

 previousAllRead boolean Optional 

 previousAllReadWrite boolean Optional 

 previousRoles string[] Optional 

 Do not change this field manually 

 primaryTerm integer · int64 Optional 

 quiet boolean Optional 

 recoveryAttempts integer · int64 Optional 

 roles string[] Optional 

 The role assigned to this investigation 

 sequenceNumber integer · int64 Optional 

 serverId string Optional 

 Holds the ID of the responsible cluster app server 

 sizeInBytes integer · int64 Optional 

 sortValues string[] Optional 

 startDate string · date-time Optional 

 startTaskId string Optional 

 FirstTask is the root task of the playbook 

 state string Optional 

 InvestigationPlaybookState indicates the state of the running playbook 

 subPlaybookInputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 subPlaybookOutputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 syncHash string Optional 

 tasks object Optional 

 Show properties

 PlaybookView represents the view in client of playbook graph

 Delete ad-hoc task 

 post https://hostname /inv-playbook/task/delete/ {investigationId} / {invPBTaskId} 

 Delete an ad-hoc task from a running playbook 

 Authorizations 

 api_key 

 Authorization string Required 

 Path parameters 

 invPBTaskId string Required 

 ad-hoc task ID 

 investigationId string Required 

 investigation ID 

 Responses 

 200 

 InvestigationPlaybook 

 application/json 

 InvestigationPlaybook is a wrapper for a linked list of Playbook tasks 

 Dirty boolean Optional 

 ReadyPlaybookInputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 ReplacedPlaybook boolean Optional 

 Indicate whether this playbook has new history during this session 

 ShardID integer · int64 Optional 

 UpdatedOperatorIDs boolean Optional 

 allRead boolean Optional 

 allReadWrite boolean Optional 

 autoExtracting boolean Optional 

 cacheVersn integer · int64 Optional 

 comment string Optional 

 created string · date-time Optional 

 dbotCreatedBy string Optional 

 Who has created this event - relevant only for manual incidents 

 hasRole boolean Optional 

 Internal field to make queries on role faster 

 highlight object Optional 

 Show properties 

 id string Optional 

 incidentCreateDate string · date-time Optional 

 Incident create date 

 indexName string Optional 

 inputs object · PlaybookInput[] Optional 

 PlaybookInputs - array of PlaybookInput 

 Show properties 

 invPBDebugInfo object · InvPlaybookDebugInfo ... Optional 

 Show properties 

 investigationId string Optional 

 isTIM boolean Optional 

 modified string · date-time Optional 

 name string Optional 

 numericId integer · int64 Optional 

 outputs object · PlaybookOutput[] Optional 

 PlaybookOutputs - array of PlaybookOutput 

 Show properties 

 pbHistory object · InvestigationPlaybookData[] Optional 

 in: body 

 Show properties 

 pendingTasks object Optional 

 Tasks that are marked for running, but cannot yet run due to incomplete parents 

 Show properties 

 playbookId string Optional 

 previousAllRead boolean Optional 

 previousAllReadWrite boolean Optional 

 previousRoles string[] Optional 

 Do not change this field manually 

 primaryTerm integer · int64 Optional 

 quiet boolean Optional 

 recoveryAttempts integer · int64 Optional 

 roles string[] Optional 

 The role assigned to this investigation 

 sequenceNumber integer · int64 Optional 

 serverId string Optional 

 Holds the ID of the responsible cluster app server 

 sizeInBytes integer · int64 Optional 

 sortValues string[] Optional 

 startDate string · date-time Optional 

 startTaskId string Optional 

 FirstTask is the root task of the playbook 

 state string Optional 

 InvestigationPlaybookState indicates the state of the running playbook 

 subPlaybookInputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 subPlaybookOutputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 syncHash string Optional 

 tasks object Optional 

 Show properties 

 version integer · int64 Optional 

 view object · PlaybookView Optional 

 PlaybookView represents the view in client of playbook graph 

 xsoarHasReadOnlyRole boolean Optional 

 xsoarPreviousReadOnlyRoles string[] Optional 

 xsoarReadOnlyRoles string[] Optional

 application/json 

 date string · date-time Optional

 InvestigationPlaybook 

 application/json 

 InvestigationPlaybook is a wrapper for a linked list of Playbook tasks 

 Dirty boolean Optional

 Show properties

 Indicate whether this playbook has new history during this session

 UpdatedOperatorIDs boolean Optional

 Who has created this event - relevant only for manual incidents

 Internal field to make queries on role faster

 Show properties

 Holds the ID of the responsible cluster app server

 Edit ad-hoc task 

 post https://hostname /inv-playbook/task/edit/ {investigationId} 

 Edit an ad-hoc task in a running playbook 

 Authorizations 

 api_key 

 Authorization string Required 

 Path parameters 

 investigationId string Required 

 investigation ID 

 Body 

 application/json 

 addAfter boolean Optional 

 addToSeparateBranch boolean Optional 

 automationScript string Optional 

 description string Optional 

 loop object · TaskLoop Optional 

 TaskLoop contains loop details 

 Show properties 

 name string Optional 

 neighborInvPBTaskId string Optional 

 playbookId string Optional 

 scriptArguments object Optional 

 Show properties 

 separateContext boolean Optional 

 tags string[] Optional 

 type string Optional 

 TaskType is the Task in the playbook context as a node 

 Responses 

 200 

 InvestigationPlaybook 

 application/json 

 InvestigationPlaybook is a wrapper for a linked list of Playbook tasks 

 Dirty boolean Optional 

 ReadyPlaybookInputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 ReplacedPlaybook boolean Optional 

 Indicate whether this playbook has new history during this session 

 ShardID integer · int64 Optional 

 UpdatedOperatorIDs boolean Optional 

 allRead boolean Optional 

 allReadWrite boolean Optional 

 autoExtracting boolean Optional 

 cacheVersn integer · int64 Optional 

 comment string Optional 

 created string · date-time Optional 

 dbotCreatedBy string Optional 

 Who has created this event - relevant only for manual incidents 

 hasRole boolean Optional 

 Internal field to make queries on role faster 

 highlight object Optional 

 Show properties 

 id string Optional 

 incidentCreateDate string · date-time Optional 

 Incident create date 

 indexName string Optional 

 inputs object · PlaybookInput[] Optional 

 PlaybookInputs - array of PlaybookInput 

 Show properties 

 invPBDebugInfo object · InvPlaybookDebugInfo ... Optional 

 Show properties 

 investigationId string Optional 

 isTIM boolean Optional 

 modified string · date-time Optional 

 name string Optional 

 numericId integer · int64 Optional 

 outputs object · PlaybookOutput[] Optional 

 PlaybookOutputs - array of PlaybookOutput 

 Show properties 

 pbHistory object · InvestigationPlaybookData[] Optional 

 in: body 

 Show properties 

 pendingTasks object Optional 

 Tasks that are marked for running, but cannot yet run due to incomplete parents 

 Show properties 

 playbookId string Optional 

 previousAllRead boolean Optional 

 previousAllReadWrite boolean Optional 

 previousRoles string[] Optional 

 Do not change this field manually 

 primaryTerm integer · int64 Optional 

 quiet boolean Optional 

 recoveryAttempts integer · int64 Optional 

 roles string[] Optional 

 The role assigned to this investigation 

 sequenceNumber integer · int64 Optional 

 serverId string Optional 

 Holds the ID of the responsible cluster app server 

 sizeInBytes integer · int64 Optional 

 sortValues string[] Optional 

 startDate string · date-time Optional 

 startTaskId string Optional 

 FirstTask is the root task of the playbook 

 state string Optional 

 InvestigationPlaybookState indicates the state of the running playbook 

 subPlaybookInputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 subPlaybookOutputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 syncHash string Optional 

 tasks object Optional 

 Show properties 

 version integer · int64 Optional 

 view object · PlaybookView Optional 

 PlaybookView represents the view in client of playbook graph 

 xsoarHasReadOnlyRole boolean Optional 

 xsoarPreviousReadOnlyRoles string[] Optional

 Task add comment 

 post https://hostname /inv-playbook/task/note/add 

 Add comment to a task 

 Authorizations 

 api_key 

 Authorization string Required 

 Body 

 application/json 

 args object Optional 

 Show properties 

 comment string Optional 

 conditions object · TaskCondition[] Optional 

 TaskCondition - contains a condition and a corresponding label
if the condition satisfied - its label is taken as the next task(s) input (e.i. the label on the edge) 

 Show properties 

 inTaskID string Optional 

 input string Optional 

 invId string Optional 

 loopArgs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 loopCondition object · ArgAtomicFilter[][] Optional 

 ArgFilter - represent a slice of atomic filters with OR condition between them (e.i. - atomic1 OR atomic2 OR ...) 

 Show properties 

 version integer · int64 Optional 

 Responses 

 200 

 InvestigationPlaybook 

 application/json 

 InvestigationPlaybook is a wrapper for a linked list of Playbook tasks 

 Dirty boolean Optional 

 ReadyPlaybookInputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 ReplacedPlaybook boolean Optional 

 Indicate whether this playbook has new history during this session 

 ShardID integer · int64 Optional 

 UpdatedOperatorIDs boolean Optional 

 allRead boolean Optional 

 allReadWrite boolean Optional 

 autoExtracting boolean Optional 

 cacheVersn integer · int64 Optional 

 comment string Optional 

 created string · date-time Optional 

 dbotCreatedBy string Optional 

 Who has created this event - relevant only for manual incidents 

 hasRole boolean Optional 

 Internal field to make queries on role faster 

 highlight object Optional 

 Show properties 

 id string Optional 

 incidentCreateDate string · date-time Optional 

 Incident create date 

 indexName string Optional 

 inputs object · PlaybookInput[] Optional 

 PlaybookInputs - array of PlaybookInput 

 Show properties 

 invPBDebugInfo object · InvPlaybookDebugInfo ... Optional 

 Show properties 

 investigationId string Optional 

 isTIM boolean Optional 

 modified string · date-time Optional 

 name string Optional 

 numericId integer · int64 Optional 

 outputs object · PlaybookOutput[] Optional 

 PlaybookOutputs - array of PlaybookOutput 

 Show properties 

 pbHistory object · InvestigationPlaybookData[] Optional 

 in: body 

 Show properties 

 pendingTasks object Optional 

 Tasks that are marked for running, but cannot yet run due to incomplete parents 

 Show properties

 previousRoles string[] Optional 

 Do not change this field manually 

 primaryTerm integer · int64 Optional 

 quiet boolean Optional

 The role assigned to this investigation

 serverId string Optional 

 Holds the ID of the responsible cluster app server 

 sizeInBytes integer · int64 Optional 

 sortValues string[] Optional 

 startDate string · date-time Optional 

 startTaskId string Optional 

 FirstTask is the root task of the playbook 

 state string Optional 

 InvestigationPlaybookState indicates the state of the running playbook

 Un complete a task 

 post https://hostname /inv-playbook/task/uncomplete 

 Reopen a closed task and change the status to uncomplete 

 Authorizations 

 api_key 

 Authorization string Required 

 Body 

 application/json 

 args object Optional 

 Show properties 

 comment string Optional 

 conditions object · TaskCondition[] Optional 

 TaskCondition - contains a condition and a corresponding label
if the condition satisfied - its label is taken as the next task(s) input (e.i. the label on the edge) 

 Show properties 

 inTaskID string Optional 

 input string Optional 

 invId string Optional 

 loopArgs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 loopCondition object · ArgAtomicFilter[][] Optional 

 ArgFilter - represent a slice of atomic filters with OR condition between them (e.i. - atomic1 OR atomic2 OR ...) 

 Show properties 

 version integer · int64 Optional 

 Responses 

 200 

 InvestigationPlaybook 

 application/json 

 InvestigationPlaybook is a wrapper for a linked list of Playbook tasks 

 Dirty boolean Optional 

 ReadyPlaybookInputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 ReplacedPlaybook boolean Optional 

 Indicate whether this playbook has new history during this session 

 ShardID integer · int64 Optional 

 UpdatedOperatorIDs boolean Optional 

 allRead boolean Optional 

 allReadWrite boolean Optional 

 autoExtracting boolean Optional 

 cacheVersn integer · int64 Optional 

 comment string Optional 

 created string · date-time Optional 

 dbotCreatedBy string Optional 

 Who has created this event - relevant only for manual incidents 

 hasRole boolean Optional 

 Internal field to make queries on role faster 

 highlight object Optional 

 Show properties 

 id string Optional 

 incidentCreateDate string · date-time Optional 

 Incident create date 

 indexName string Optional 

 inputs object · PlaybookInput[] Optional 

 PlaybookInputs - array of PlaybookInput 

 Show properties 

 invPBDebugInfo object · InvPlaybookDebugInfo ... Optional 

 Show properties 

 investigationId string Optional 

 isTIM boolean Optional 

 modified string · date-time Optional 

 name string Optional 

 numericId integer · int64 Optional 

 outputs object · PlaybookOutput[] Optional 

 PlaybookOutputs - array of PlaybookOutput 

 Show properties 

 pbHistory object · InvestigationPlaybookData[] Optional 

 in: body 

 Show properties 

 pendingTasks object Optional 

 Tasks that are marked for running, but cannot yet run due to incomplete parents 

 Show properties 

 playbookId string Optional 

 previousAllRead boolean Optional 

 previousAllReadWrite boolean Optional 

 previousRoles string[] Optional 

 Do not change this field manually 

 primaryTerm integer · int64 Optional 

 quiet boolean Optional 

 recoveryAttempts integer · int64 Optional 

 roles string[] Optional 

 The role assigned to this investigation 

 sequenceNumber integer · int64 Optional 

 serverId string Optional 

 Holds the ID of the responsible cluster app server 

 sizeInBytes integer · int64 Optional 

 sortValues string[] Optional 

 startDate string · date-time Optional 

 startTaskId string Optional 

 FirstTask is the root task of the playbook 

 state string Optional 

 InvestigationPlaybookState indicates the state of the running playbook 

 subPlaybookInputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 subPlaybookOutputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 syncHash string Optional 

 tasks object Optional 

 Show properties 

 version integer · int64 Optional 

 view object · PlaybookView Optional 

 PlaybookView represents the view in client of playbook graph 

 xsoarHasReadOnlyRole boolean Optional 

 xsoarPreviousReadOnlyRoles string[] Optional 

 xsoarReadOnlyRoles string[] Optional

 Complete a task 

 post https://hostname /v2/inv-playbook/task/complete 

 Complete a task with command and multiple file attachments 

 Authorizations 

 api_key 

 Authorization string Required 

 Body 

 multipart/form-data 

 investigationId string Optional 

 investigation ID 

 taskId string Optional 

 Task Id 

 taskComment string Optional 

 Task comment or command to run 

 taskInput string Optional 

 Task input 

 version string Optional 

 Version 

 file string Optional 

 Files to attach to the task 

 fileNames string Optional 

 file names separated by %###% (only if files provided) 

 fileComments string Optional 

 file comment separated by %###% (only if files provided) 

 Responses 

 200 

 InvestigationPlaybook 

 application/json 

 InvestigationPlaybook is a wrapper for a linked list of Playbook tasks 

 Dirty boolean Optional 

 ReadyPlaybookInputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 ReplacedPlaybook boolean Optional 

 Indicate whether this playbook has new history during this session 

 ShardID integer · int64 Optional 

 UpdatedOperatorIDs boolean Optional 

 allRead boolean Optional 

 allReadWrite boolean Optional 

 autoExtracting boolean Optional 

 cacheVersn integer · int64 Optional 

 comment string Optional 

 created string · date-time Optional 

 dbotCreatedBy string Optional 

 Who has created this event - relevant only for manual incidents 

 hasRole boolean Optional 

 Internal field to make queries on role faster 

 highlight object Optional 

 Show properties 

 id string Optional 

 incidentCreateDate string · date-time Optional 

 Incident create date 

 indexName string Optional 

 inputs object · PlaybookInput[] Optional 

 PlaybookInputs - array of PlaybookInput 

 Show properties 

 invPBDebugInfo object · InvPlaybookDebugInfo ... Optional 

 Show properties 

 investigationId string Optional 

 isTIM boolean Optional 

 modified string · date-time Optional 

 name string Optional 

 numericId integer · int64 Optional 

 outputs object · PlaybookOutput[] Optional 

 PlaybookOutputs - array of PlaybookOutput 

 Show properties 

 pbHistory object · InvestigationPlaybookData[] Optional 

 in: body 

 Show properties 

 pendingTasks object Optional 

 Tasks that are marked for running, but cannot yet run due to incomplete parents 

 Show properties 

 playbookId string Optional 

 previousAllRead boolean Optional 

 previousAllReadWrite boolean Optional 

 previousRoles string[] Optional 

 Do not change this field manually 

 primaryTerm integer · int64 Optional 

 quiet boolean Optional 

 recoveryAttempts integer · int64 Optional 

 roles string[] Optional 

 The role assigned to this investigation 

 sequenceNumber integer · int64 Optional 

 serverId string Optional 

 Holds the ID of the responsible cluster app server 

 sizeInBytes integer · int64 Optional 

 sortValues string[] Optional 

 startDate string · date-time Optional 

 startTaskId string Optional 

 FirstTask is the root task of the playbook 

 state string Optional 

 InvestigationPlaybookState indicates the state of the running playbook

 Show properties

 Complete a task 

 post https://hostname /v2/inv-playbook/task/form/submit 

 Submit a data collection task with given answers and multiple file attachments 

 Authorizations 

 api_key 

 Authorization string Required 

 Body 

 multipart/form-data 

 investigationId string Optional 

 investigation ID 

 taskId string Optional 

 Task Id 

 answers string Optional 

 the answers to the task form. Answers are keyed by numerical question id 

 file string Optional 

 Files to attach to the task 

 Responses 

 200 

 InvestigationPlaybook 

 application/json 

 InvestigationPlaybook is a wrapper for a linked list of Playbook tasks 

 Dirty boolean Optional 

 ReadyPlaybookInputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 ReplacedPlaybook boolean Optional 

 Indicate whether this playbook has new history during this session 

 ShardID integer · int64 Optional 

 UpdatedOperatorIDs boolean Optional 

 allRead boolean Optional 

 allReadWrite boolean Optional 

 autoExtracting boolean Optional 

 cacheVersn integer · int64 Optional 

 comment string Optional 

 created string · date-time Optional 

 dbotCreatedBy string Optional 

 Who has created this event - relevant only for manual incidents 

 hasRole boolean Optional 

 Internal field to make queries on role faster 

 highlight object Optional 

 Show properties 

 id string Optional 

 incidentCreateDate string · date-time Optional 

 Incident create date 

 indexName string Optional 

 inputs object · PlaybookInput[] Optional 

 PlaybookInputs - array of PlaybookInput 

 Show properties 

 invPBDebugInfo object · InvPlaybookDebugInfo ... Optional 

 Show properties 

 investigationId string Optional 

 isTIM boolean Optional 

 modified string · date-time Optional 

 name string Optional 

 numericId integer · int64 Optional 

 outputs object · PlaybookOutput[] Optional 

 PlaybookOutputs - array of PlaybookOutput 

 Show properties 

 pbHistory object · InvestigationPlaybookData[] Optional 

 in: body 

 Show properties 

 pendingTasks object Optional 

 Tasks that are marked for running, but cannot yet run due to incomplete parents 

 Show properties 

 playbookId string Optional 

 previousAllRead boolean Optional 

 previousAllReadWrite boolean Optional 

 previousRoles string[] Optional 

 Do not change this field manually 

 primaryTerm integer · int64 Optional 

 quiet boolean Optional 

 recoveryAttempts integer · int64 Optional 

 roles string[] Optional 

 The role assigned to this investigation 

 sequenceNumber integer · int64 Optional 

 serverId string Optional 

 Holds the ID of the responsible cluster app server 

 sizeInBytes integer · int64 Optional 

 sortValues string[] Optional 

 startDate string · date-time Optional 

 startTaskId string Optional 

 FirstTask is the root task of the playbook 

 state string Optional 

 InvestigationPlaybookState indicates the state of the running playbook 

 subPlaybookInputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 subPlaybookOutputs object Optional 

 Show properties 

 syncHash string Optional 

 tasks object Optional 

 Show properties 

 version integer · int64 Optional 

 view object · PlaybookView Optional 

 PlaybookView represents the view in client of playbook graph 

 xsoarHasReadOnlyRole boolean Optional 

 xsoarPreviousReadOnlyRoles string[] Optional 

 xsoarReadOnlyRoles string[] Optional
